HUD/UI: ability glyph strip, portrait (shipwreck graveyard), stage (shipwreck bog), talent icons, three body costumes baked with `tools/costume/wreckwitch_bake.py` from a repainted 4-view sheet (DROWNED SIREN, BOG QUEEN, FROZEN WRECK) and one icon sheet. FX: painted atlas `assets/fx/wreckwitch.png` (brine, kelp, bilge cloud, broken heal plus, chain, whirlpool, drowned-hand ring, bilge puddle...) plus HQ paintings for the puddle, hand ring, whirlpool and cloud, and a themed atlas per costume (pearl/coral, peat bog, ice).
- Passive TIDE RISING (`hero/wreckwitch.ts`, `HeroState.tide`): each second within 6 m of a living enemy champion adds a stack (max 10); 4 s without one and they ebb one per 0.5 s; death clears them. Per stack +5% damage (`damageMulOf`), 2% less damage taken (defender scaling), 1.5% of damage dealt drunk back as health, and the model grows 3% (eased in `heroSync`). The concept's +3% damage alone lost every 1v1 (2-30 over all 8 champions): she can't kite to build stacks, so the stacks had to keep her alive while she brawls. Bot "hover at 4.5 m to build stacks first" also tested worse than just fighting and was dropped.
- A ANCHOR SWING: 2-hit combo 56 / 80 (160° / 200°, 2.8-3 m). Holding A whirls instead: while `charging === "a"` (which already slows to 45%) a 360° sweep every 0.35 s (22, 2.9 m) for up to 3 s; the release still powers the next swing like anyone's charge.
- B DREDGE (8 s): anchor down the aim line up to 9 m (stops at tall terrain), first non-structure foe caught (champions preferred); after the chain's flight time (`later`) 80 damage, 0.5 s stun, 60% slow 1.5 s, and the two swap places (she lands where they stood, they land 0.9 m from her old spot toward the throw; no swap through sealed gates, onto void, or on CC-immune targets). UNDERTOW synergy (`synergy.dredgeStack`, caster: 2): dragging a foe that's already slowed or stunned while she has a caster partner gives 2 Tide stacks.
- R BILGE (10 s, placeable 5 m): 60 damage in 3 m and a 3 s brine cloud (`Zone.style "bilge"`, 16 dps, 75% slow); everyone hit or standing in it gets `status.noHealUntil` +3 s, honoured by `World.heal` (all heals, kegs, leech, lingering heals) and by home/rest regen in `freeMove`.
- Z DAVY'S GRIP: drowned hands in a 6 m ring round her: 70 + 10 per Tide stack, rooted 1.5 s (slow 0, no knockback); buildings take half.
- L+X CHAIN SWING: within 3.5 m of a building (preferred) or a blocked cell (tree, rock, wall), the dodge becomes a 0.5 s invulnerable arc ~150° around it toward the stick (`HeroAction.kind "chainswing"`, tries 120-175° and a wider radius until it finds open ground); otherwise a normal dodge.
- Evolutions: R Brine Squall (+1 m, 5 s cloud, stuns 0.8 s) / Scurvy (26 dps, 40% slow, +15% damage taken from her house inside); B Keelhaul (+40, 1 s stun; with Brine Squall leaves a bilge puddle on the catch) / Long Chain (13 m, -2 s, +2 Tide per champion caught; with Scurvy the catch can't heal 3 s); A Barnacle Crust (+20 shield per hit up to 120, champion hits add a Tide stack once a second) / Maelstrom (second swing x1.35 + 0.5 s stun, whirl +1 m, x1.4, pulls); Z Davy's Locker (+2 m, 2 s, 13 per stack).
- Bot (`witchFight` in bot/tactics.ts): Davy's Grip on 2 champions, at 5+ stacks or for a kill; Bilge on healers, then anyone mending / low / in reach; Dredge the enemy marksman/caster out of their backline (or chase with it), but not into their partner or a tower; whirl only in a crowd of soldiers. Results (4 games per matchup on crossing, 0.8 skill): 1v1 14-18 overall (Grim 4-0, Warlord 3-1, Stig / Francois / Maddock 2-2, Wren 1-3, Remnil / Thorn 0-4) where the same harness has Thorn 9-19 and Warlord 7-21; 2v2 with Remnil 4/16 against four teams (Thorn+Remnil 5/16); team deathmatch plays normally (4/3, 3/4 K/D).
- PROFESSOR HOOT, the Snow Architect (`architect`, builder; docs/new-champions.md). A round owl-folk scholar from a flooded frost temple: tawny barred plumage, brass ice-lens goggles, charcoal quilted coat with snow-white fur, blue team scarf and sleeve bands, an ice-block pack on his back, a giant carpenter's square. 1.85 m. Art: Nano Banana turnaround (Stig + Maddock sheets as style refs, one A-pose/proportion fix edit) -> Tripo P1 body (14.4k tris); one Tripo call for a 3-prop row (square, snow fort block, ice lookout tower) split by gaps and islands (`tripo_heroes/_architect_split.py`). `tripo_heroes/architect.py`: hand skinning like Maddock (coat on the hips, pack rigid on the chest, satchel/slate on the hips), the square in hand_R as `architect_square` (hidden while thrown, never merged), own clips: two flat square swings + a hopping chop (attack_a/b/c), a sidearm boomerang `throw`, a two-handed stamping `build` (forts / lookout, also `slam`), a wing-flinging `cast` (dome) and a flapping-hop `dodge`. Props `snowfort` (one wall cell), `lookout` (2x2 tower, platform at 1.9 m, scaled to the deck height and footprint at runtime) and `architect_square` (the flying square) in `build_tripo_props.py` (new optional `yaw`). Costumes SNOWY (snowy owl, plum velvet, ice pack), SUNKEN TEMPLE (slate-green, jade robes with gold glyphs, mossy temple-stone pack), CLOCKWORK (brass automaton, oxblood leather, boiler pack) via the usual repaint-and-bake (`tools/costume/architect_bake.py`), one icon sheet for all four heads, painted portrait (flooded frost temple), stage, ability glyph strip, 7 evolution icons. FX: own 4x4 atlas `assets/fx/architect.png` (snowflake, ice shard, snow, spray, frost ring, icicle, ice block, chalk, blueprint, ruler swoosh, frost burst, twinkle, snow brick, feather, drafting circle, cracked ice) plus a themed sheet per costume (`architect@snowy|temple|clockwork`: violet frost; temple glyphs, kelp, shells and sea water; gears, rivets, springs and steam), and HQ paintings for the big ones (`hq/architect.{iceCrack,frostRing,spray,drafting}[@costume]`: the dome from above, the frost ring decal, the avalanche burst, the blueprint circle). Costume tints, trails, swaps and chunk skins in `costumeSkins.ts`. Voice: the adventurer pack pitched up (`vo.architect.*`, `tools/sfx/pitchcast.sh` from the cut engineer lines; also declared in manifest.py). fal: ~$4.30 (18 Nano Banana, 2 Tripo).
- HOOT kit (`src/sim/hero/architect.ts`; sturdy health, mid speed, high damage, bot range 2.2). VANTAGE (`hooks.highGroundMul` 1.2, `highGroundHeight` 0.9): hits 20% harder from at least 0.9 m above the target; FEATHERFALL (`hooks.featherFall`): no fall damage/stun from knockback drops. A: square combo 50/50/84; a charged A (>= 25% charge) throws the square as a boomerang (`w.boomerangs`, style "square", 44 damage, 9 m, x1.4 range at full charge, 2.2 s), A while it's out recalls it. B SNOW FORT (`kind: fort`, 9 s): a curved 5 m ice wall (a wall terrain mod of style "ice" along an arc bowed toward him, placeable 8 m) for 12 s with a 220 hp anchor structure (the works pattern: breaking it ends the wall), max 2 (the oldest melts), shoves bodies out, 50 + 0.5 s stun to foes caught in it; allies within 4 m behind it take x0.6 from hits sourced more than 4 m away on the far side (`fortCoverMul`). R LOOKOUT (`kind: lookout`, 15 s): a 2x2 deck mod (style "lookout") 2 m above the highest ground under him, raised under his feet (searching up to 2 cells around), allies lifted, foes shoved off; no ramp, so only jumps reach it; 300 hp anchor; one at a time; 10 s. Z AVALANCHE DOME (`kind: dome`): a 5.5 m zone (`Zone.dome`) for 6 s: 70 + 0.8 s stun + slow to foes inside when it lands, foes inside slowed to 55%, allies mend 30/s, and `World.damage` rejects any non-tick hit from more than 3.2 m away whose source and target are on opposite sides of the rim (a "domeBlock" ice spark where it breaks). No rng is used, so other matches stay bit-identical (determinism check passes). OWL HOP (dodge): hop onto his lookout within 6.5 m, else up a ledge 0.6-3.2 m higher 2-4.6 m ahead, else over one of his forts in front of him. Synergy: with a marksman partner the lookout is 3x3 (`synergy.lookout`). Evolutions: R Icicle Belfry (the lookout's anchor becomes a siege shooter: 40 every 1 s, 9 m, shot style "icicle"; +4 s, +100 hp) / Avalanche Drop (the lookout bursts for 90 + 0.7 s freeze when it ends; R again topples it); B Ice Bastion (+2 m, x1.6 hp, 3 forts; with Belfry cover x0.4) / Frost Fort (60 + 0.6 s freeze blast as it rises; with Avalanche Drop forts shatter for 60); A Long Measure (throw x1.4 range, x1.3 speed, 0.4 s daze, -2 s fort cooldown per champion hit) / Frostbite (combo and square slow 25%, x1.15 vs slowed); Z Glacier Dome. Bot (`architectFight`/`architectEscape` in bot/tactics.ts): lookout when a melee champion closes within 5 m or 4+ melee soldiers crowd him, Owl Hop back up when knocked off, forts toward ranged champions 5-13 m away (or between him and a melee chaser when hurt), half-charged square throws at 4.5-9 m (full charge from the perch), the dome on ranged champions in reach or melee ones toe to toe, and stepping off the perch toward the fight or home (the tower has no nav path down). `Bot.chargeAt` (default 1) lets a bot release a charge early. TDM style: skirmisher. FFA DM mods hp x1.1, damage x1.15.
- Hoot 1v1 bot sims (8 games per opponent, crossing/ruins/shoals/hollow, sides swapped, skill 0.8): 30/64 (47%): Warlord 3-5, Stig 4-4, Grim 4-4, Remnil 1-7, Francois 6-2, Thorn 7-1, Wren 3-5, Maddock 2-6. Remnil out-ranges him (the bot doesn't yet dome him early enough). FFA deathmatch (6 eight-house games) and 2v2 Hoot+Wren (3x3 lookout seen) ran without errors.
- Gristle, the Cellar Boar (`vintner`, tank): a huge grey boar-folk vintner, 2.1 m. Nano Banana turnaround (Maddock/Wren/Stig refs, one A-pose fix edit and one front-only A-pose edit) → Tripo P1 body (14.4k tris); one Tripo call for both props (chain anvil weapon + strapped back anvil on one sheet, split at the gap in `tools/blender/tripo_heroes/vintner.py`). Hand skinning like Maddock (apron/mail skirt on the hips), the chain anvil rigid in hand_R (`vintner_anvil`, PCA-oriented pommel → head), the big anvil rigid on the chest (`vintner_backanvil`, shares the 512 prop texture). Own clips: planted hunched idle, heavy backhand (attack_a) and two-handed overhead (attack_b/c) timed to the combo hitAts, `pound` (charged A), `headbutt` (lowered-head rush), `cast` (beckoning Switcheroo), `crush` (three overhead slams at 0.55/1.05/1.62 s of 2 s), `dodge` = the Anvil Curl crouch, `block` = anvil across the body.
- Gristle's kit (`src/sim/hero/vintner.ts`): THICK SKIN (`HeroState.grit` 0..1, builds over 2 s while his stick velocity is ~0 or he blocks, drains over 1.5 s while walking/rushing/rolling; up to 25% less damage in `defenderScaling`); A two-hit combo 60/95 that doesn't break Grit, held to ≥ half charge it becomes ANVIL POUND (`startPound` converts the started combo action: 90 in 3.4 m, knockback 11, 0.35 s stun); B HEADBUTT (6 m rush, cc-immune while rushing; the first enemy champion is carried in front of him, and if they stop against a wall/cliff/structure/sealed gate both stop and the champion is PINNED: +60 and 1.1 s stun; soldiers bowled aside; without a wall the carried champion is shoved off the end); R SWITCHEROO (swap with the ally champion in most trouble within 8 m or the one the stick points at, 120 shield for 3 s; `synergy.switchAlly` gives marksman/caster partners ×1.6 shield + 30% speed for 2 s; with no ally in reach it swaps with the nearest enemy champion within 6 m and dazes them 0.45 s); Z CRUSH SEASON (three slams, 45%/65%/100% of 130, radius 60%/78%/100% of 4.4 m, the last stuns 1.2 s and hits buildings for 400; cc-immune throughout); ANVIL CURL replaces his dodge (a short 0.36 s low roll, ends with his back to the nearest enemy champion; for 1.2 s hits from behind are blocked to 15%, `curlUntil` in the damage pipeline). Evolutions: Stomp In (swap lands with a stomp) / House Rules (+3 m, 200 shield, −3 s); Battering Ram / Thick Head (armorOnUse 40% + cc immunity); Iron Hide (Grit 35% in 1 s) / Trample (overhead finisher ×1.25, stun 0.5 s); super Vintage Year (+1 m slams, the last leaves a 4 s crushed-grape `wine` zone). Bot (`vintnerFight` in bot/tactics.ts): plants his feet in reach so Grit builds, Headbutts champions with a wall within 2.5 m behind them (or low runners, occasionally to close a gap), pounds with a charged A at full Grit or into 3+ soldiers, Switcheroos a partner below 45% with a champion on them (or below 25%), jukes a fleeing low champion when alone, curls when a ranged champion is mid-cast or a super is coming. FFA deathmatch mods hp ×1.15, speed ×1.08, damage ×1.22.
- Gristle balance (bot sims, 0.8 skill): first pass (damage mid, speed ×0.86, Grit over 3 s) won 5/64 1v1s (Thorn's kit swapped in for comparison: 19/56); damage tier high, speed ×0.96, Grit over 2 s, Headbutt 75/+60 cd 6 s, Pound 90 → 33/80 1v1 (4 maps: Warlord 7-3, Grim 8-2, Maddock 5-5, Thorn 4-6, Stig 4-6, Francois 3-7, Wren 2-8, Remnil 0-10: kited by ranged as designed); 2v2 23/64 (best with Remnil 8/16); 8-bot FFA DM after mods: 2/12 wins, K/D 15.1/13.2 (fair share 1.5).
- Gristle art: FX atlas `assets/fx/vintner.png` (grapes, vine leaf, wine splash/drop/puddle, forge sparks, iron shard, chain link, cork, crater, dust ring, grit shield, swap arrows, dizzy grapes, crushed-grape splat; cut by `tools/fx-prompts/vintner_fx_cut.py`, which rounds the crater tile) and HQ ground paintings `assets/fx/hq/vintner.{puddle,crater,splat,swap}.png`. Kit `src/render/kits/vintner.ts`: anvil slams = crater + dust ring decal + sparks + clods + wine (the final Crush Season slam adds the splat), dizzy grapes over a pinned champion, gold swap rings at both ends of Switcheroo, Grit-full stone shield pop; the HUD draws a stone shield beside the Z ring that fills with Grit. Costumes HARVEST KING (russet boar, golden harvest apron, bronze scales), FORGEMASTER (sooty black, ember cracks, copper), ICEWINE (white snow boar, icy teal apron, silver): body repaint sheets baked with `tools/costume/vintner_bake.py`, each with its own unique FX atlas (`assets/fx/vintner@<costume>.png`), tints and trail colours. Costume icons from one sheet (`tools/costume/vintner_icons.py`), ability glyph strip, portrait (moonlit vineyard), stage (vineyard wine cellar at night), 7 evolution icons (`tools/fx-prompts/vintner_talents_cut.py`). Voice: the orc pack at ×0.7 with a 3.8 kHz low-pass (`vo.vintner.*`, also in the sfx manifest); anvil clangs, wall-pin crunch and bell, swap blink. fal: ~$3.70 (17 Nano Banana, 2 Tripo).
- Bramble & Mead, the Honey Rider (`rider`, support, from `docs/new-champions.md`). Art: Nano Banana turnaround of a badger-folk courier riding a pony-sized bumblebee (Stig/Wren/Maddock sheets + portraits as style refs) and one painted 3/4 A-pose view -> Tripo P1 (14k tris); props sheet (honey ladle + honey pot) -> one Tripo mesh split by the empty gap (`keep`). `tools/blender/tripo_heroes/rider.py`: the shared humanoid rig with the bee, saddle and her legs rigid on `hips` (so the mount bobs, pitches and banks with it), torso banded over hips/spine/chest/head, arms by distance to the chain, and two extra bones `wing_L`/`wing_R` (children of hips) that every clip buzzes; arm rest pose held out and forward. All clips are authored for a seated rider: hover idle, run (pitched forward, fast buzz), forehand/backhand ladle sweeps (attack_a/b), overhead whack (attack_c), left-hand pot throw (`throw`), overhand honey `fling`, two-arm rising `cast` (Take Wing / Royal Jelly), `fly`, sideways banking `dodge`, `hit`, toppling `death`. Ladle (Tripo, SVD-aligned) in hand_R; `assets/props/honeypot.glb` is the thrown pot. Hero height 1.9 m.
- Kit (`src/sim/hero/rider.ts`). Passive SWEET TOOTH: she and friends within 7 m regenerate (champions 0.7%/s, soldiers 2%/s, herself 0.9%/s), doubled for anyone standing 0.75 m+ above whoever last hit them (4 s memory). A: two-hit ladle combo (50 / 72, wide second hit); a charged release (held >= 30%) becomes LADLE FLING instead: a ballistic honey dollop 8.5 m, 55 + splash, slows 40% for 2 s. B HONEY POT: lobbed pot (`w.later` landing, no new projectile list), 45 damage (x2 on soldiers), heals friends 80, then a 3 m `honey` zone for 4 s: friends heal 30/s, foes slowed 45% and STUCK (new `Zone.sticky` -> `status.stickyUntil`, dodge input is dropped with a STUCK IN HONEY callout). Synergy `honeyAlly`: a bruiser or assassin partner standing in her pool gets x1.2 damage (brew buff, 2 s linger). R TAKE WING: `hero.wing` flight state handled before the normal controller (like a jump): 3 s steered at 1.3x speed over walls, water, cliffs and gaps (only map bounds and sealed gates stop her), invulnerable and cc-immune, sim height never drops below take-off (no chasm deaths); R again lands after 0.45 s; landing snaps to the nearest walkable cell, heals friends within 4 m for 70, then 1.5 s of 20% extra damage taken and a 0.4 s landing lag (the counterplay). Not usable while carrying the Grudge outside deathmatch, and picking it up mid-air lands her. Z ROYAL JELLY: friends within 10 m heal 220 (soldiers half), 120 shield for 4 s, slows cleansed, +25% speed for 5 s. L+X BUZZ STRAFE: her dodge is always a sideways buzz (any stick direction, 1.15x a roll's time and 1.1x its speed) that keeps her facing. Talents: Stinger Dive / Tailwind (R), Beeswax (+Stinger Dive: landing pool) / Swarm Pot (+Tailwind: longer pool) (B), Second Helping / Honey Drizzle (A), Queen's Feast (Z). FFA deathmatch mods hp 1.2, speed 1.15, damage 1.6 (`ffaHeroMods`).
- CPU Bramble (`riderFight` in bot/tactics.ts): Honey Pot onto a champion in reach when they're close or mid-swing, charged honey flings at 3-8 m, Royal Jelly when she or a partner is under half, Take Wing out of a losing brawl and onto kiting ranged champions; `escape: "r"` in retreat. Sims (bots 0.8): 1v1 vs every champion on crossing/ruins 21/48 (44%; best vs Wren 5-1, worst vs Grim/Thorn/Stig/Francois/Maddock 1-2 of 6), 28% over 4 maps before the last buff; 2v2 with Warlord / Grim partners 3-1, Thorn 2-2, Francois 0-4; FFA deathmatch mid-pack after the mods. Determinism baseline unchanged; repeated Bramble matches hash identically.
- Bramble visuals: kit `src/render/kits/rider.ts` (honey splash hits, honey-blob projectile, the pot prop on an arc with drips, splash + shards + pool decal, lift-off gust, landing bloom, Royal Jelly crowned honeycomb ring with bees and jelly, buzz streaks, Sweet Tooth pollen, high-ground bee, partner buff), `heroProps/rider.ts` (eased flight lift with pollen/petals, `fly` clip, no gold ward outline while flying) and a `honey` zone style (HQ pool decal, comb pieces, drops, circling bees). New painted atlas `assets/fx/rider.png` (16 cells) + HQ `rider.pool/ring/splash/gust`. Costumes (texture repaints of the body, `tools/costume/rider_*`): WAR HORNET (`warhornet`, black lacquer and crimson hornet), LAVENDER FIELDS (`lavenderfield`, sage smock, lilac bee), QUEEN'S COURIER (`queencourier`, purple velvet and gold queen bee), each with its own FX atlas, HQ paintings (Hornet keeps the atlas gust), honey tints and trail colour; four Smash-style head icons from one sheet. Glyph strip, painted portrait (orchard, ladle raised, Mead peeking in), stage (orchard apiary), 7 talent icons, codex pages (+ BUZZ STRAFE combo page, live demos incl. a steered Take Wing), voice cast from the RPG Voice Starter Pack type 1 at 0.86, `bramble.honey` squelch (slime pack), synth wing buzz, no footsteps. fal: ~$3.95 (17 Nano Banana incl. 2 costume retries, 2 Tripo).
- Merged the six new champions (hero/harpooner, scribe, wreckwitch, architect, vintner, rider): talent id clashes renamed (Kelp's MAELSTROM -> BREAKERS, Brindle keeps MAELSTROM and UNDERTOW), synergy typed as a free-form partner-class table, codex glyphs moved to a CHAMP_GLYPH map, demo ability sets unioned. Champion select lays out 14 champions on two rows of smaller cards (seats start below the roster).
- Loading splash repainted with all 15 characters (Nano Banana Pro edit from an in-engine action-shot board of every champion + the Herald; prompt in tools/fx-prompts/loading_art14_prompt.txt): the Herald dead centre with his banner, the 14 champions in a V on a jousting field at dusk. Old 9-champion art kept as assets/generated/loading/art9_raw.png.
- 14-champion balance pass (per-hero `hooks.heroDamageMul`, damage vs champions only): Wren 0.88, Remnil 0.92, Warlord 1.25 -> 1.33, Gristle 1.1, Bramble 1.1, Hoot 1.12, Brindle 1.2; Francois's caster synergy removed (Francois + caster pairs ran 80%). 1v1 round robin (364 games) after the first step: 31-62% (Wren 62 ... Warlord 31; Warlord stays low in 1v1 but his 2v2 comps are strong). 12 logical 2v2 comps (264 games): 34-64% apart from Hollin+Francois (80%, fixed by the synergy removal); match length 3.6-5.2 min, K/D and structures built similar across comps, sudden death 26%.
- Champion select announcer: sealing a champion plays the announcer calling their full name (ElevenLabs eleven-v3 via fal, one voice "Brian", per-champion delivery through v3 audio tags - e.g. BROTHERRR... MADDOOOCK in a deep jolly rumble, MOTHERRR... KELP cackling). `tools/gen-announcer.mjs` writes assets/sfx/name.<hero>.0.ogg (silence-trimmed, loudnorm -14 LUFS); raw mp3s in assets/generated/announcer. Checked with fal whisper transcripts. ~$0.03.
- Loading splash rebuilt from the real models (AI group shots kept drifting off-model: wrong beards, missing champions): `tools/splash-shots.mjs` renders every champion mid-action with Portraits.actionShot (Herald: attack_b thrust with the banner up), a painted empty jousting-field backdrop (Nano Banana, tools/fx-prompts/loading_backdrop_prompt.txt), and `tools/splash-compose.py` composites them - Herald front and centre and biggest, eight across the front, six on a higher back row in the gaps so every face shows, warm dusk grade and contact shadows. Inputs in assets/generated/loading/splash/.
- Loading splash (third try): five small-group paintings, three champions each (Nano Banana Pro edit from a reference sheet of each trio's painted portrait + in-game model, so they stay on-model; prompts in tools/fx-prompts/loading_group_g*.txt), shown as five gold-framed cards fanned across the screen - the Herald's trio (Stig, Herald, Hoot) in the middle, biggest and in front - on a blurred, darkened copy of his painting, the top left clear for the logo (`tools/splash-strips.py`). The all-15 single paintings drifted off-model and the composite of in-game renders looked wrong; both dropped.
- Announcer redone on ElevenLabs Eleven v4 (was v3): short one-breath calls without "..." pauses, two takes each keeping the tighter one, inner pauses squashed to 0.12 s (0.6-1.7 s per call), checked with fal whisper (stretched spellings that came out wrong - Gristle, Kelp, Wren, Bramble - respelled, Bramble via IPA). A new call, picking a seal back up, B, going to field select, starting a match or leaving to the menu cuts the previous call (Sfx.stopName). Opening gate lockdown 30 s -> 15 s (warning 4 s).
- Fix: the six-champion union merge left two stacked `else if (a.name === "dodge" ...)` lines in heroSync, the first with an empty body, so every ordinary dodge played no clip (champions slid along the ground). Merged into one condition (excluding Maddock's keg rocket, Hollin's page gust and Kelp's chain swing, which have their own clips). Scanned the merged files for other stacked branches and duplicate case labels: none.
- Mother Kelp chain-swing indicator: `chainSwingPlan` (pure, shared with startChainSwing) tells where L+X would swing right now; for local Kelps with the dodge ready a teal ring marks what the anchor would hook, dots trace the arc and a ring marks the landing (`src/render/swingHint.ts`). Bramble: A while flying (Take Wing) drops a honey bomb straight down every 0.7 s (32 damage, slow in 2.2 m, a short sticky patch, no healing); CPU Bramble steers over targets and bombs whatever is under her (41 bombs over 16 flights in 3 sims). Hoot's carpenter's square scaled down (SQUARE_SCALE 1.75 -> 1.0, hero rebuilt).
- Avalanche Dome is one-way: the dome's own house shoots in and out freely, only enemy shots are stopped at the rim. Hoot: up to 3 Snow Forts (cooldown 9 -> 6 s) and 2 Lookouts (oldest falls first; cooldown 15 -> 9 s, lasts 14 s); CPU Hoot also raises a Lookout in a shootout with a ranged champion within 11 m, or a second one when his first is out of hop reach (it only perched against melee / soldiers, so against Hollin it never got high ground). CPU Gristle closes the gap on a ranged champion backing off: Headbutt whenever he's out of swing reach (was a 12% roll), and alone he Switcheroos with a kiter he can't reach once Headbutt is down. 4-game 1v1 checks: Hoot 15/24 (beats Hollin 3-1), Gristle beats Remnil 4-0 (was 0/10), still loses to Wren and Hollin.
- Ability charges (`AbilityDef.charges`, HeroState.stock, hero/common.ts spendCharge / refundCharge): uses that recharge independently; the slot cooldown is when the next charge is ready. Hoot's Snow Fort (2 charges, 8 s each, up to 3 forts standing) and Lookout (2 charges, 12 s each, 2 standing): B B or R R back to back from the start. Melee CPU reflex (`meleeReflex`, after every think): a melee champion not retreating and with nothing else decided swings at the nearest enemy soldier / champion in reach - chasing kiters, walking to objectives or sieges they used to walk past soldiers in their face (Gristle swung 2% of the time with enemies in reach). Gristle vs Wren / Hollin 0-4 -> 2-2 in 4-game checks.
- Passive / synergy logic pass. Grim: Lone Wolf (30% leech + 30% armour vs soldiers/towers when alone) removed - it rewarded what his kit already does - and his partner `edge` synergy removed (a loner buffing partners made no sense). In its place Vanish (`killHeal` 0.3, `killResetsR`): a champion kill heals 30% and readies Smoke (Leap already reset). Soldiers hit him 15% harder (`soldierTakenMul`). Combo stabs never flinch (`AbilityDef.noFlinch`), so foes can walk out. Execute Dash takes no backstab or Smoke-ambush bonus (it scales only off the target's missing health; a full-hp Maddock could be one-shot by ambush x2 * flank x1.5 * crit * 180); Smoke ambush x2 -> x1.6; CPU Grim no longer opens with the dash from Smoke. Warlord: Unbreakable keeps -40% taken when two champions are on him, drops the +30% damage. Stig: `edge` -> Bastion (`synergy.bastion`): a marksman / caster (0.85) or support (0.88) partner within 7 m of one of their buildings takes less. Kelp: Dredge synergy stacks now come from a bruiser / tank partner (was caster). Bramble: high-ground double heal removed; Pollen (`pollen*` hooks): Mead's buzz dodge dusts a friendly champion it passes - +20% speed, +15% damage for 3 s; CPU Bramble buzzes through a partner squaring up. Hoot: Vantage-anywhere -> Watchtower (`watchMul`, `watchRadius`): anyone of his house on top of one of his lookouts hits 20% harder, and foes within 9 m of a lookout can't hide in grass, mist or smoke. Sims after: 1v1 Grim 21% (was 62%; the leech was most of his CPU strength), Hoot 69%, rest 33-63%; 2v2 Grim comps 25-27%, others 43-68%.
- Grim gets leech back, smaller and unconditional: Bloodthirst (`heroLeech` 0.2, damage dealt to champions, no lone-wolf condition, no armour). Soldiers hit him 10% harder (was 15%). 1v1 CPU checks with 15-22% leech: 19-25%; the old 62% came mostly from the Smoke-ambush Execute Dash burst, which stays removed.
- Class glyphs (`assets/ui/glyphs/class_<class>.png`, Nano Banana Pro edit with ui_glyphs_raw as the style reference: fist = bruiser, riveted shield = tank, dagger + moon = assassin, bow = marksman, orb staff = caster, heart + cross = support, hammer + wrench + bricks = builder; tools/gen-class-glyphs.mjs + tools/slice-class-glyphs.py, raw sheets in assets/generated/class_glyphs_*_raw.png). Champion select: cream glyph on a dark disc in each portrait tile's bottom-left corner, and in front of the champion's name on the seat card (`classGlyph` in selectArt.ts).
- Champion select: SIT HERE on a CPU seat card moved off the stage (it covered the costume strip in 4-player) onto the ability-glyph row at the bottom of the card, which it replaces while shown.
- SIT HERE rethought: no longer a button laid over the card. A CPU seat's plaque row is now SIT | CPU | level gems (SIT where a human seat has the pencil), on the big 1v1/2v2 cards and the compact deathmatch cards alike; hover tip SIT HERE / TAKE THIS SEAT. Nothing on the stage, costume strip, trees or ability row is covered.
- SIT is a painted glyph button like the pencil / camera: a tavern stool (`assets/ui/glyphs/sit.png`) for a local pad, a chair (`take.png`) for an online guest taking the seat, same size and padding as the pencil, so a CPU row reads [stool] CPU [gems] like [pencil] PLAYER [camera]. gen-class-glyphs.mjs / slice-class-glyphs.py take ad-hoc glyph lists on the command line (raw sheet assets/generated/glyphs_sit_raw.png).
- Champion select ghost chips: seats past the mode's seat count (5-8 after leaving an 8-player deathmatch) kept their CPU champion and their chips were still drawn - unpickable, since the seat isn't in play. `Screens.championSeat` now returns false for seats beyond the mode (2 in 1v1, 4 in ffa/2v2, all in tdm/ffadm), so they draw no chip.
- Costume repaints for every hero prop / weapon that lacked one (30 Nano Banana Pro edits, ~$5): each hero's prop sheet (4 views per prop, exp.py renders) repainted with the hero wearing the costume as reference, projected back with bake2.bake (`tools/costume/prop_costumes.{json,mjs,py}`). New: Hoot square (held and thrown, `prop_architect_square`), snow fort, lookout; Brindle bow, back harpoons, fired harpoon; Bramble ladle, honey pot; Hollin quill; Gristle anvil; Kelp anchor (held and thrown), drowned hand; Remnil hex idol, tomb; Thorn wall stone; Stig palisade; Warlord quake spike. `propParts(name, costume, hero)` now applies the owner's costume texture to instanced props (forts, lookouts, walls, tombs, spikes); a `prop_<material>.jpg` wins for runtime props whose material name matches a held weapon with different UVs; Kelp's anchor and hands pass her costume.
- Grim: NIGHTSHADE (purple hood, green skin - read as his classic look) replaced by FROSTBITE, a snow goblin kept dark for contrast: cold blue-teal skin, midnight-navy hood and tattered cloak with white fur trim and frosted icicle hem, slate quilted tunic, charcoal leathers with silver buckles, navy/ice leg wraps, fur-cuffed boots, icy-cyan vial; frosted crystal-edged knives; icy trail 0x9ae8ff; recoloured stock icon (`raider_frostbite.png`). A first all-white fur pass was dropped (it would read as a white blob on snow). Prompts in tools/costume/raider_frostbite_*_prompt.txt.
- Themed FX for all 27 recolour costumes of the original champions (Warlord, Stig, Grim, Remnil, Francois, Thorn, Wren, Maddock, Herald), which used to show classic effects: a 4x4 atlas `assets/fx/<atlas>@<costume>.png` each, and for heroes with big HQ paintings (Warlord impact/rage, Grim smoke/vortex, Remnil burst/circle/hex, Thorn natureBurst/rune/wisp/bramble zone, Wren arrowRing/spiral, Maddock blast/hopRing/puddle/splash) themed `assets/fx/hq/<id>@<costume>.png` at the base painting's size (512/1024). 45 Nano Banana Pro edits (~$7), the costumed hero render as theme reference (tools/fx-prompts/old_costume_fx.{mjs,py}, themes in old_costume_themes.json). HQ paintings are generated on a green screen (glows painted into magenta came back pink), magenta for green themes (hopmaster, plague, swamp), with despill and interior-colour fill for soft glow edges. Kit colours drawn over the cells (rings, glows, streaks) are re-hued per costume and every costume has a weapon-trail colour (`RECOLOUR` in costumeSkins.ts). Thorn's Blossom atlas was regenerated once (first pass came back grey).
- Warlord quake fissures follow his recolours: `lava@{bloodmoon,gilded,swamp}.png` (assets/fx/lava.png palette-mapped by tools/fx-prompts/lava_variants.py: blood-red, molten gold, toxic green) as the molten core, with matching cut/lip colours, through COSTUME_SKIN.<costume>.fisMat (the path the Colossus seam already used). They were orange lava in every recolour.
- Gristle's anvil hammer remodelled: the old weapon was cut out of the shared props sheet and decimated, so its anvil was small, distorted and hard to read. New Nano Banana concept (big classic anvil head: flat face, long horn, heel, pinched waist, stepped foot; chained oak shaft, leather grip, brass pommel; tools/blender/tripo_heroes/vintner_hammer_prompt.txt) -> its own Tripo P1 model (assets/source/vintner_hammer_tripo.glb, 3k -> 2.4k tris), 1.25 m in hand (was 1.15). The back anvil still comes from the props sheet. Costume repaints (forgemaster, harvestking, icewine) regenerated for the new UVs. ~$0.8 fal.
- HUD passive slot: a framed box after the Z ring on the player panel for champions whose passive has a live state, with painted glyphs (`assets/ui/glyphs/passive_*.png`, Nano Banana Pro via gen-class-glyphs.mjs, raw glyphs_passive_raw.png): Wren Vantage (bow-eye on a ledge, lit when on), Gristle Grit (cracked stone shield, fills), Kelp Tide (wave, stack count), Hollin Runes (quill rune, runes standing), Hoot Watchtower (tower + eye, lit when perched, lookouts standing). Gold frame when full or lit. Replaces the old vector diamond / shield beside the Z ring. Charged abilities (Hoot B / R) show banked uses in a gold badge on the button. slice-class-glyphs.py splits at the drawn panel dividers (panels come back uneven).
- HUD: charged abilities show one yellow pip per banked use under B / R (was a number badge); the passive box sits further from the Z ring. Costume gaps fixed: wall / fort crumble FX (Hoot snow forts, Thorn and Stig walls) ran outside any costume - now under the owner's (stored on the wall view); wall meshes are built under it too (moss tufts etc. via cm()); Hoot's ice chunks use tint(ICE); Stig's tesla glow sprite takes the costume's arc cell and colour. Audit of every kit import outside src/render/kits: zones, snares, projectiles, hero props (pip, keg rider, scribe bolts, bee lift) already resolve the owner's costume; generic structure build / death puffs are shared by design.
- Hoot Clockwork: the atlas "snow" cell (the puff of fort / lookout rises, crumbles and avalanches) was painted bright white steam; recoloured to sooty brass steam (umber -> khaki) to match the brass/copper forts. Clockwork ice-chunk colour 0xd8f4ff -> 0xc8a060 (was pale cream). Hoot's trail colour mapped per costume.
- Mother Kelp chain swing is now a HELD dodge: a tap of L+X (or the dodge key) is always a plain roll (fired on release); holding it past 0.2 s shows every hookable pivot in reach (faint rings: structures, plus the nearest blocked cell per 45-degree sector, `swingPivots`), the stick picks one (bright ring, arc, landing marker) and releasing swings round it in the stick direction. `Command.swing` carries the pivot; `startChainSwing` only fires with it. CPU Kelp keeps swinging round the nearest pivot when it dodges next to one. Smash-flick dodges stay plain rolls.
- Chain swing review. It missed most things because the pivot search skipped every cell with infinite standable height - which is exactly what walls and solid props are - so only cliffs / slopes and buildings counted. `swingPivots` now hooks: buildings (any side, neutral), every solid map prop (trees, rocks, towers, statues, carts...), tall walk-through props (trees, pines, apple trees, yews, dead trees, columns, spires, statues, obelisks, torches, banner poles, broken pillars, angels, cacti, topiaries), Hoot's lookouts, and wall cells - map walls and hero walls (stone walls, palisades, snow forts), nearest per 45-degree sector. Not cliffs, slopes or water. Walkable spots with something in reach: crossing 64%, gardens 93%, ruins 82%, hollow 81%. Swings whack whoever she passes through (`swingDamage` 30, knockback 7, once each per swing) and chain: another held swing within 0.9 s of landing continues the chain (up to 5, `swingChain` / `swingChainWindow`), ignoring the dodge cooldown; a fresh swing or a full chain puts the dodge on cooldown.
- Balance: Hoot square 44 -> 38. Maddock powder keg: 2 charges, flies 0.35 -> 0.2 s and the throw is quicker (dur 0.5 -> 0.36), cooldown 6 -> 8, damage 85 -> 75; R detonates only once no charge is left (first test with 2 fast kegs and no other change put him at 71%). Bramble honey bombs 32 -> 24. Grim: 2 Smoke charges (not Leap: the Double Jump talent already recasts Leap); `heroDamageMul` 1.3 (champions only - the leech / burst nerfs left him at 4-21%). CPU Grim: `clearFirst` (cuts down nearby soldiers, archers first, before diving a champion who still has soldiers within 6 m and is above 40%), hit-and-run (`huntRatio` 0.9, `hitAndRun` 2), Smoke as a second escape (`escape2`). Death analysis: he wasn't being caught while fleeing - he lost straight melee trades within ~2 s.
- Hoot: one Snow Fort (no charges, 1 standing, cooldown 9 s); Lookout keeps 2 charges. Grim Double Jump is now Leap with 2 charges (two pips, independent recharges) instead of a 3 s recast; resets (kills, Shadow Step) give back one charge (`refillCharge`).
- Hollin redesigned as GRANNY HOLLIN, THE NOVELIST (two bee-themed champions was one too many; Bramble keeps the bees). New Nano Banana design edited from her old turnaround so proportions, A-pose and the long-coat silhouette carry over: silver bun with pencils and a feather, half-moon spectacles on a beaded chain, plum cable-knit cardigan-coat with gold stars / moons / little dragons, cream ruffled blouse, royal-blue knitted shawl (team dye), book-buckle belt, chained manuscript and ink pots; Tripo P1 body (14.4k tris) on her existing rig, skinning and clips; the quill stays. Prompts: tools/blender/tripo_heroes/scribe_novelist_prompt.txt, sheets in assets/generated/scribe_novelist_*.png.
- Her old look is now a 5th MODEL costume, BEEKEEPER (`scribe@beekeeper`, tools/blender/tripo_heroes/scribe_beekeeper.py): the original body (assets/source/scribe_bee_tripo.glb) with a new giant HONEY DIPPER staff (Nano Banana concept -> Tripo, scribe_dipper_prompt.txt) instead of the quill; it keeps the old bee FX atlas and HQ paintings (`scribe@beekeeper.png`, `hq/scribe.*@beekeeper.png`) and the buzzing swarm sounds. scribe.py's reskin now reads CFG["src"] (it always re-imported scribe_tripo.glb).
- Three genre recolours replace Queen Bee / Vigil / Red Ink: CHILDREN'S AUTHOR (buttercup knit, patchwork, ducklings and ABCs, crayons in her bun, red picture book), MYSTERY (houndstooth tweed, green velvet cuffs, cameo, magnifying glasses and keys, casebook), SCI-FI (midnight navy with glowing cyan circuits, rockets and planets, chrome tablet, moon boots); body + quill repaints (tools/costume/scribe_*_body_prompt.txt, scribe_bake.py, prop_costumes.json), FX atlas + 4 HQ paintings each (old_costume_fx pipeline), kit tints and trails, five new stock icons (scribe_novelist_icons.py).
- New base FX: bees, honey and honeycomb gone - paper birds, an open glowing spellbook, a ring of whirling pages, a star-and-moon rune seal, a dragon flourish, an illuminated border with moons and dragons (tools/fx-prompts/scribe_novelist_fx_prompt.txt) and matching HQ paintings (scribe_novelist_hq_prompt.txt). B is now PAPER STORM (pages and paper birds; same mechanics), Royal Hive -> EPIC LENGTH, Stinging Prose kept with paper wording; new B glyph, two talent icons, portrait (writer's study), stage (novelist's tower study), codex lore / role / ability text, announcer "Granny Hollin!", and her card on the loading splash (g4 regenerated with the new look). Paper Storm sounds are page flutters (bees only for the Beekeeper).
- Weapon grips: Tripo hands are one rigid bone with straight fingers, so weapons floated in flat palms. `curl_grip_fingers` (build_tripo_hero.py) bends the finger half of each weapon hand round the grip in the rest pose - two knuckles at 42% / 70% of the hand bone, 52 degrees each toward the palm, scaled by the hand weight; applies to every hand an attached prop is bound to (CFG grip / curl / knuckles override). All 24 hero and costume models rebuilt. Gristle's anvil hammer now hangs head-down from his fist (WEAPON_AXIS (0, -0.55, -1)) instead of jutting sideways. Review sheets: /tmp/opencode/grip (idle / attack / run close-ups before and after).
- Grip seating (`snap_grips`, build_tripo_hero.py, runs before the finger curl): each long held weapon (principal axis >= 2.2x the next) is shifted sideways - perpendicular to its own axis, so the hold point along it is kept - until the axis passes through the centre of the hand (mean of the hand-weighted body verts), capped at 0.12 m; per hand for one-mesh pairs (Grim's knives). Bulky props (tankard, square, anchor, Calliope key) and props with extra bones (bows with a string bone) are left as placed. Shifts: Hollin quill 0.21 (capped), Francois rapier 0.084, Stig wrench 0.085, Sporeblight knives 0.1, Remnil staff 0.05, Shadowplay staff 0.075, Warlord club 0.04. All 24 models rebuilt.
- Hand orientation: open hands whose palm faced out / forward are twisted about the hand bone toward the body (`orient_palms`, CFG["palm_twist"] per side, picked by eye on hand review renders; PALM_TWIST env to try values): Maddock and Celadon left +70, Hoot left +60, Kelp left +60, Suntotem both 60. It runs after the attachments (several attach steps re-skin the body from the source and would undo it). An automatic version (flattest axis of the hand as the palm normal) was tried and dropped: on fist-like Tripo hands the palm side is ambiguous, and it flipped Stig's and Francois' hands. The finger curl now bends toward the measured palm side (`palm_normal`).
- Maddock powder keg: `chargeGap` 2 - even with the second charge banked, the next keg waits 2 s after a throw (`AbilityDef.chargeGap`, spendCharge). R detonates only when no charge is banked, so pressing R during the gap does nothing rather than blowing the first keg.
- Kelp chain swing: reach 3.5 -> 5.5 m; holding L now hops - each landing immediately swings round the next pivot the stick points at (input re-picks every tick from the live pivot list), up to 5 swings, then the dodge rests 4 s (`swingChainCooldown`); a tap is still a roll and a held swing that can't land never turns into a roll. A swing goes the other way round when the stick's side has nowhere to land, and only pivots with a landing are offered.
- Stig: health tier sturdy (980) -> mid (800); Ballista (Z) damage 95 -> 75, hp 520 -> 380.
- CPU fixes found by per-matchup diagnostics (/tmp/opencode/wsim/diag.ts):
  - Targeting: ranged shots and targeted spells re-picked their target at fire time from the swing direction, so soldiers in front soaked them (Remnil put 84 of 300 shots on Wren while "targeting" her). The CPU's chosen target is now sent as Command.aimAt, locked onto the action as it starts (HeroAction.aimId) and used at fire time (`aimFor`, also Brindle's harpoon aim). Human aiming unchanged.
  - Unreachable champions: `sense` only considered enemy champions it could walk to, so ranged CPUs ignored Hoot on his lookout. Ranged CPUs now take any visible champion within their range + 5 m.
  - Harpoons at a raised target fly at its height (they stuck in the side of the lookout).
  - Ranged CPUs kite melee soldiers that close within 3.2 m (step back from them, roll out of two or more) - fragile shooters were dying mostly to soldiers.
  - Ranged CPUs don't fire into an enemy dome (shoot something else) and walk round a snow fort covering their target (`domeShields`, side-effect-free).
  - Francois' parry read: 0.9 x skill -> 0.5 x skill per readable swing (72% -> 40% at default skill); melee CPUs hold their swing while his parry window is open.
- 1v1 grid (10 games per matchup, Hollow + Ruins): 9-1-or-worse matchups 21 -> 14, within 6-4: 38 -> 43 of 91; spread Hoot 65% .. Grim 35% (was Stig 68% .. Grim 35%).
- WOUNDED (new, data/match.json `wound`): any hit from a ranged champion (botRange > 3: Wren, Remnil, Hollin, Brindle) cuts the target champion's healing to 60% for 2.5 s (a dark red flash when it lands). Diagnostics with DPS + heal/s showed healers out-healing ranged DPS in 1v1 (Wren 40 DPS into Maddock healing 30/s; Bramble 13-37/s). Maddock's passive regen (`selfRegen`) 0.006 -> 0.003 (his heal/s breakdown vs Wren: Plenty 8.5, ale puddles 6.5, keg splashes 6.4, Brewfest 2.7, rest 2.2).
- Hoot: lookout Watchtower bonus x1.2 -> x1.1, champion damage bonus x1.12 removed (with ranged CPUs now able to target him on the lookout he hit 70%).
- Grid after (10 per matchup, Hollow + Ruins): Gristle 63, Hoot 58, Francois 55, Remnil 54, Maddock 52, Thorn / Wren / Brindle / Kelp 50, Warlord 49, Hollin 48, Bramble 44, Stig 43, Grim 34. Finding for later: kills don't decide 1v1s, armies do - e.g. Wren out-damages Gristle 3x and trades kills evenly but loses because his crush / pound wipes her soldiers (her army 1-2 vs his 10-14 at the 1-minute mark).
- Smarter soldiers (units.ts, every army, human- or CPU-ordered): target spreading (a target already taken by more friends than it needs - 2 for a soldier, 4 a champion, 6 a building - scores worse, so a group splits over the enemies in reach); archers kite (an enemy melee soldier or champion within 2.2 m while reloading: step back 2.5 m); packs (a marching soldier with no friend within 5 m but marching friends behind within 25 m waits for them, at most 4 s at a time); flanking (a push of 8+ with no lane ordered sends every third soldier down the lane farthest from the main body first); screen (following a ranged champion, grunts and brutes stand 3.5 m toward the nearest threat). CPU orders by class (bot/strategy.ts classDirectives, one per-type order per tick): assassins push grunts + brutes as a second front from 4 soldiers while archers follow; builders keep archers on defend after the first minute; others as before. Isolated each class plan in a 130-game row: assassin plan Grim 30% vs 25% without, Francois flat; builder plan Stig 37% vs 34% - kept. Wren vs Gristle (army wiped by the 1-minute mark before) now 2-2 in a 4-game check.
- Grid after (10 per matchup): Remnil 63, Francois 63, Hoot 62, Gristle 62, Thorn 56, Kelp 53, Maddock 51, Wren 48, Brindle 46, Hollin 46, Warlord 41, Stig 39, Bramble 39, Grim 31; 22 matchups at 9-1 or worse (the army changes reshuffled matchups - more tuning needed: Grim, Stig, Bramble low; Remnil / Francois high).
- Stig health tier stout (900, new tier between mid 800 and sturdy 980). C-stick orders redone: up ATTACK (lane cycling as before; big pushes flank on their own), left GUARD (`screen` directive: grunts and brutes stand 3.5 m between the champion and the nearest threat, archers 1.5 m behind), right SPLIT (an order, not a state: on the army, grunts + brutes attack while archers guard; on one group, it attacks), down DEFEND. HOLD dropped from the cross (still used by commanders / CPUs). New painted badges order_screen / order_split (raw assets/generated/hud_orders_guard_split_raw.png); codex orders page rewritten.
- 9-1 matchups categorized (8-game reruns with kills / DPS on champion / heal per second / army size): seed noise (3), healer out-heals the damage (4: Maddock vs Stig / Wren / Bramble, Bramble vs Wren), supports lose melee duels badly (6: Francois / Thorn / Kelp vs Maddock and Bramble, Francois vs Grim), casters kite slow melee tanks and win the army (3: Remnil vs Thorn / Warlord, Hollin vs Gristle), Hollin loses ranged duels (2), assassin ground down on the map (1: Stig vs Grim). Changes: Maddock Healing Keg heal 110 -> 85, puddle heal 22 -> 14; Wounded also applies on any champion's big hit (finishers, slams, dashes); Bramble's honey pool slows to 45% (was 55%); Maddock's CPU retreats at 45% (Bramble's stays 30% - 45% made her passive, 33% -> 41%); Remnil hex bonus vs soldiers removed (hexMinionMul 1.3 -> 1.0), raised dead 5 -> 3; Hollin ink bolt 56 -> 62 damage, speed 22 -> 28; Grim health tier lean (700, new). Grid: 9-1-or-worse 19 -> 12, within 6-4 42 of 91; Gristle now top (71%).
- Kelp held swing never hooks the same anchor twice in a row within a chain (`swingFrom`, `sameAsLast`: not offered by placeRanges, refused by startChainSwing).
- Gristle damage breakdown (8 games): combo 43%, Crush Season 25% (10 of 28 killing blows), Headbutt 18%, Pound 12%. Crush Season 130 -> 105 per slam, stun 1.2 -> 0.8 s; his x1.1 champion damage bonus removed (71% -> 52% in his row).
- Hollin vs Wren: not a gap problem - she outranges Wren (12.5 vs 10 m) and targets her, but CPU Hollin only charged bolts when nobody had hit her for 2 s, i.e. never in a shootout, so Wren's Vantage shots out-damaged her 2:1. She now charges whenever no melee soldier or champion is within 6 m: the charged blot blinds (half of Wren's shots miss) and writes runes - Wren's damage on her fell ~30%. With that, her earlier ink bolt buff overshot (61%); back to 56 damage, speed 24 (was 22). Row 50%, Hollin vs Wren 3-7 (was 0-10 / 1-9).
- Grim champion damage x1.3 -> x1.38 (row 43%).
- Grid: Francois 68, Stig 58, Remnil 57, Hoot 57, Gristle 53, Wren 52, Thorn 48, Grim 46, Maddock 46, Kelp 46, Brindle 45, Hollin 45, Warlord 40, Bramble 38; 9-1-or-worse 11, within 6-4 46 of 91.
- Outlier pass. Francois vs Grim: Grim's Execute Dash was fired from the edge of its 10 m reach (missed ~2 in 3) and by the generic Z rule into soldiers or after the target died; it now fires only at a living champion within 7 m who is executable or killable (raiderFight returns true, so the generic rule never fires it). Grim also stops chopping soldiers when a champion is within 4 m of him (clearFirst dive). Francois's damage came from combo 35%, Lunge 27%, Blade Flurry 21% (10 of 24 killing blows): Flurry 45 -> 38 per hit (68% -> 61%).
- Bramble: CPU Honey Fling was nearly never thrown - the bot held A for a full 1 s charge but re-rolled the hold every think, so most holds came out as plain swings. It now releases at flingCharge and keeps a started hold; a melee champion slowed by her honey is kited at ~6 m and flung at. Plus fling 55 -> 70, combo 50/72 -> 56/80 (38% -> 45%).
- Warlord: CPU spends War Cry to run down a kiting ranged champion within 4-12 m (Wren 2-8 -> 5-5). War Cry speed x1.12 -> x1.2, Ground Slam cooldown 7 -> 6 s, champion damage x1.33 -> x1.42 (40% -> 46%).
- Grid: Francois 61, Hoot 58, Stig 58, Remnil 55, Thorn 53, Wren 52, Gristle 52, Hollin 47, Warlord 46, Brindle 45, Bramble 45, Kelp 44, Maddock 43, Grim 40; 9-1-or-worse 11, within 6-4 44 of 91.
- Grim vs Francois deep dive (8 instrumented games): Francois won the spacing - his swings reach 3.3 m against Grim's 2.4 m stabs; Grim's Leap (0.5 s windup) was dodged or parried 24 times of 43 (parry -> 0.8 s stun -> Flurry took him 89% -> 18% in one exchange); and the CPU dodged Leaps from a smoked, invisible Grim. Fixes: reactToCasts only reacts to a caster it can see; Grim's stabs reach 2.8 m (finisher 3.0); botPlan `stand` (Grim): with his escape Leap down and a no-healthier champion within 3 m he fights on (leech) instead of turning his back. Holding the Leap while Francois's parry was up tested worse (3-17), as did x1.15 Flurry-on-stunned (no change). Grim row 51%, vs Francois 8-12 over 20 (was 4-16).
- Sampled 2v2 (/tmp/opencode/wsim/duo.ts): 21 duos, every champion in 3, 84 matchups x 10 games on Hollow/Ruins.
- 2v2 role diagnostic (/tmp/opencode/wsim/duodiag.ts: champion damage share, kills, assists, deaths, healing by source per champion over its sampled duo games). Hollin leads, not helps: 59% of her team's champion damage and 56% of its kills, 2.4 deaths. Bramble healed herself ~2.5x more than her partner (her pots landed on her own melee target). Stig died most (6.8 / game): his team's buildings draw both enemy champions (foes' building damage 571 / min in his games against 359) and he dies defending them. Kelp died alone (6.2), her CPU swing always hooked the nearest anchor, often flinging her back at the chaser.
- New `match.duo` (2v2-only: a team fielding two or more champions, World.duo): Hollin champion damage x0.8; Stig takes x0.88 damage and his team's towers have x1.35 health; Bramble's healing on allies x1.4. CPU Bramble throws Honey Pot onto a hurt partner who has a foe on them; CPU Kelp retreating swings round the anchor that lands her farthest from the nearest enemy champion (or just rolls).
- Sampled 2v2 after: Hollin 61, Wren 59, Hoot 57, Grim 54, Bramble 54, Francois 52, Thorn 50, Gristle 48, Maddock 48, Warlord 48, Stig 44, Brindle 43, Remnil 43, Kelp 42 (was 70 ... 40). 1v1 grid: 58 ... 42, 9-1-or-worse 12, within 6-4 44 of 91.
- 2v2 teamwork (CPU). Baseline metrics (/tmp/opencode/wsim/group.ts, 77 games): mates 10.9 m apart, within 12 m in 69% of fight moments; 21 deaths / game, only 45% with the mate within 12 m, 26% caught alone by both enemy champions (two thirds of those after the mate had died), 67% of kills shared. Changes: a retreating champion runs to a healthy partner within 20 m (unless that's back through the chaser) instead of home; an attacker's assist reach is 32 m (was 22) when the partner is below 55%; regroup(): caught alone by both enemy champions with the local fight against us, back off toward the partner (or home) unless finishing a nearly dead foe - tanks exempt (they're the rally point; without the exemption Thorn fell 50% -> 36%); an attacker with nothing to hunt joins a partner out in the field 16+ m away. Leaving earlier once the mate died was tried and did nothing (the survivors were already retreating). After: deaths 18 / game, 71-73% kills shared. Bramble's 2v2 ally heal x1.4 -> x1.2 (her heals now land, 61%). 2v2: Grim 56, Hollin 55, Francois 55, Wren 54, Bramble 54, Maddock 53, Warlord 52, Hoot 50, Gristle 48, Stig 46, Brindle 45, Kelp 45, Thorn 44, Remnil 43; within 60-40 41 of 84. 1v1 unchanged.
